## IAH-ʽEN DJÜN FOH-ING SHÜ. <u>约翰</u>传福音书

### Iʽe. I.

<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>DAO</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>起初</rb><rt>kyʽi-tsʽu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>万物</rb><rt>Væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造个</rb><rt>zao-go</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>造出来个</rb><rt>zao-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造个</rb><rt>zao-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>活命</rb><rt>Weh-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>暗个</rb><rt>en-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>照东</rb><rt>tsiao-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>暗个</rb><rt>en-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>得知</rb><rt>teh-cü</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>亮光个</rb><rt>Liang-kwông-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>cong-nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>*te-tsing*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>亮光个</rb><rt>Liang-kwông-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>*læ-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>真个</rb><rt>Tsing-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>造个</rb><rt>zao-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>*di-fông*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自个</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>生出来</rb><rt>*sang-cʽih-læ*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>血脉</rb><rt>hyüih-mah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>肉身个</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>生出来</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 26. 耶（稣）后（头）26（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, gyün-ping. 或话，权柄。（原注）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>Dao</rt><rb>成</rb><rt>dzing</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>亲眼</rb><rt>*tsʽing-ngæn*</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin-Vu*</rt><rb>差来</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt><rb>独养</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*Ng-ts*</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>；）<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>满足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>后如</rb><rt>ʽEo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊重了</rb><rt>tseng-djong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>满足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>*eng-we*</rt><rb>得着了</rb><rt>teh-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>*teh-djôh*</rt><rb>恩上</rb><rt>eng-zông</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>恩</rb><rt>eng</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>传个</rb><rt>djün-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恩惠</rb><rt>eng-we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>托</rb><rt>tʽôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>独养</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>*Jing-ming*</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>祭司</rb><rt>tsi-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>利未</rb><rt>Li-vi</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>赖</rb><rt>la</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。」「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>回报</rb><rt>we-pao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>Ziang</rt><rb>先知</rb><rt>sin-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以赛亚</rb><rt>Yi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>旷野</rb><rt>Kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>铺直</rb><rt>pʽu-dzih</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>。』 <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>*sing-hyiang*</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 30. 耶（稣）后（头）30（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以利亚</rb><rt>Yi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得个</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>后如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊重了</rb><rt>tseng-djong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>连</rb><rt>lin</rt><rb>解</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>鞋带</rb><rt>ʽa-ta</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>欠</rb><rt>kyʽin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯大巴喇</rb><rt>Pah-da-pô-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>顶勒去个</rb><rt>ting-leh-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>！<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>后如</rb><rt>ʽeo-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>尊重了</rb><rt>tseng-djong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>特为</rb><rt>deh-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>显</rb><rt>hyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>鹁鸽</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天里</rb><rt>tʽin-li</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。』<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看见过了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>Di-nyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>*dô-kô*</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>*læ-kæn*</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>Siao-yiang</rt></ruby>！」<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>跟的</rb><rt>keng-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>拉比</rb><rt>Lah-pi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>庉间</rb><rt>deng-kæn</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>？」（<ruby><rb>拉比个</rb><rt>Lah-pi-go</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>。）<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉过</rb><rt>deng-ko</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽô-pun-nyih</rt><rb>毛</rb><rt>mao</rt><rb>四</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, tæn-kôh-kæn. 或话，耽搁间。（原注）</sup>

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>跟</rb><rt>keng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>个</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>弥赛亚</rb><rt>Mi-sæ-üô</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>弥赛亚</rb><rt>Mi-sæ-üô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>翻出来</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。）<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>矶法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u>。」（<u><ruby><rb>矶法</rb><rt>Kyi-fah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>翻出来</rb><rt>fæn-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup>。）

<sup>\[1\] Kyi-toh *go* *ka-shih*, *ziu-z* fu-yiu Cü-kwu. <u>基督</u>个解说，就是傅油主顾。（原注）</sup>

<sup>\[2\] Pe-teh *go* *ka-shih*, *ziu-z* zah-deo. <u>彼得</u>个解说，就是石头。（原注）</sup>

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>跟</rb><rt>Keng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯赛大</rb><rt>Pah-sæ-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>、<ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>个</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>本城</rb><rt>peng-dzing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但业</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>约瑟</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿但业</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>拿撒勒</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>好个</rb><rt>hao-go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但业</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>点着</rb><rt>tin-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>实惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>奸诈个</rb><rt>kæn-tsô-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿但业</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>无花果</rb><rt>vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树下</rb><rt>jü-ʽô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>拿但业</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>以色列</u>个</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>无花果</rb><rt>Vu-hwô-ko</rt><rb>树下</rb><rt>jü-ʽô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见其</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从今以后</rb><rt>Dzong-kying-yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>开开</rb><rt>kʽæ-kʽæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>人个儿子</rb><rt>Nying-go-Ng-ts</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Iʽe. II.

<ruby><rb>第三</rb><rt>DI-SÆN</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦拿</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一份</rb><rt>ih-veng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>好日</rb><rt>hao-nyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>请去间</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyʽi-kæn</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>好日酒</rb><rt>hao-nyih-tsiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>Tsiu</rt><rb>欠了</rb><rt>kyʽin-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>呒得了</rb><rt>m-neh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>Tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>六口</rb><rt>loh-kʽeo</rt><rb>石水缸</rb><rt>zah-shü-kông</rt><rb>安间</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每口</rb><rt>me-kʽeo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>𣥼</rb><rt>tsi</rt><rb>两三担</rb><rt>liang-sæn-tæn</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>*shü*</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>𣥼满</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>水缸</rb><rt>shü-kông</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>候沿</rb><rt>ʽeo-yin</rt><rb>𣥼满了</rb><rt>tsi-mun-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>舀出来</rb><rt>iao-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮去</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮去了</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>尝着</rb><rt>zông-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>舀</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>总管</rb><rt>tsong-kwun</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt></ruby>，<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>Nying-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>摆</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>吃足快</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-tsoh-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>推扳点</rb><rt>tʽe-pæn-tin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>留</rb><rt>liu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>头一起</rb><rt>deo-ih-kyʽi</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦拿</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉落</rb><rt>deng-lôh</rt><rb>呒几</rb><rt>m-kyi</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>鹁鸽</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>兑</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>绳</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>牛</rb><rt>ngeo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>倒掉</rb><rt>tao-diao</rt><rb>兑</rb><rt>de</rt><rb>铜钿</rb><rt>dong-din</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>翻倒</rb><rt>fæn-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>柜头</rb><rt>gyü-deo</rt></ruby>，<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>鹁鸽</rb><rt>beh-keh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>东西</rb><rt>tong-si</rt><rb>驮勒去</rb><rt>do-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>买卖个</rb><rt>ma-ma-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>着急煞</rb><rt>dziah-kyih-sah</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>？」<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拆掉</rb><rt>tsʽah-diao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>*sing*-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>造</rb><rt>Zao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>四十</rb><rt>s-jih</rt><rb>六</rb><rt>loh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>三</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>起</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>*keh-kyü*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>肌身个</rb><rt>kyi-sing-go</rt><rb>殿</rb><rt>din</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>才方</rb><rt>*dzæ-fông*</rt><rb>记得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>逾越</rb><rt>Yü-yüih</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做出来个</rb><rt>tso-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>靠托</rb><rt>kʽao-tʽôh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>*i-s*</rt></ruby>，<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>心意</rb><rt>sing-i</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。
