### Iʽe. III.

<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>重生出来</rb><rt>djong-sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-teh *fæn*, dzong zông-deo ka sang-go. 有得翻，从上头介生个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>老了</rb><rt>lao-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>生出来</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>重入</rb><rt>djong-jih</rt><rb>娘胎</rb><rt>nyiang-tʽæ</rt><rb>生出来</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>生出来</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>重生出来</rb><rt>djong-sang-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>*keh-kyü*</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>奇怪</rb><rt>gyi-kwa</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>风</rb><rt>Fong</rt><rb>随</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>吹</rb><rt>cʽü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>声响</rb><rt>sing-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>\[1\] Yiu-teh *fæn*, dzong zông-deo ka sang-go. 有得翻，从上头介生个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>做勒来</rb><rt>tso-leh-læ</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>以色列</rb><rt>Yi-seh-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>！<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>ah-lah-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落来个</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>天上</rb><rt>tʽin-zông</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇梗</rb><rt>keh-kwang</rt><rb>蛇</rb><rt>dzô</rt><rb>提上去</rb><rt>di-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>提上去</rb><rt>di-zông-kyʽi</rt></ruby>；<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> 「<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>甚至于</rb><rt>zing-ts-ü</rt><rb>赐落</rb><rt>s-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>独养</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>定罪</rb><rt>ding-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>独养</rb><rt>doh-yiang</rt><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>Ng-ts-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*Gyi-lah*</rt><rb>定罪个</rb><rt>ding-ze-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>：<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>暗</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>过如</rb><rt>ko-jü</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶个</rb><rt>ôh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>非礼</rb><rt>fi-li</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>耽搁</rb><rt>tæn-kôh</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒冷</rb><rt>Sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>哀嫩</rb><rt>Æ-neng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>挈落</rb><rt>kyʽih-lôh</rt><rb>牢监</rb><rt>lao-kæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb><u>约翰</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>扳驳</rb><rt>pæn-poh</rt><rb>弄</rb><rt>long</rt><rb>洁净</rb><rt>kyih-zing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约但</rb><rt>Iah-dæn</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>河外</rb><rt>ʽo-nga</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>话过了</rb><rt>wô-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>新来新妇</rb><rt>sing-læ-sing-vu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>新郎</rb><rt>sing-lông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>新郎个</rb><rt>sing-lông-go</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>听东</rb><rt>tʽing-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>新郎个</rb><rt>sing-lông-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>满足了</rb><rt>mun-tsoh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>大起来</rb><rt>do-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>小拢去</rb><rt>siao-long-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>凡百个</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>凡百个</rb><rt>væn-pah-go</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>受</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一颗</rb><rt>ih-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>表明</rb><rt>piao-ming</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒穷尽个</rb><rt>m-gyüong-dzing-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin-Vu*</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>万物</rb><rt>væn-veh</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>𢛟惶</rb><rt>ô-wông</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Iʽe. IV.

<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>多如</rb><rt>to-jü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u>，<sup>2</sup> （<ruby><rb>实为</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>洗礼</rb><rt>si-li</rt></ruby>。）<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>Lu</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>叙加</rb><rt>Jü-kyüô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>相近个</rb><rt>siang-gying-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>雅各</u>个</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh-go</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt><rb>走勒</rb><rt>tseo-leh</rt><rb>吃力了</rb><rt>kyʽih-lih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇口</rb><rt>keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>Shü</rt><rb>依点</rb><rt>i-tin</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」（<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。）<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」（<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来去个</rb><rt>læ-kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>。）

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>恩赐</rb><rt>eng-s</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>『<ruby><rb>依点</rb><rt>I-tin</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吃吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>』<ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>Jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>feh-jün</rt><rb>讨</rb><rt>tʽao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>吊梁桶</rb><rt>tiao-liang-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>*ka*</rt><rb>深</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇口</rb><rt>Keh-kʽeo</rt><rb>井</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>雅各</rb><rt>Yüô-kôh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>牲口</rb><rt>sang-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>水源</rb><rt>shü-nyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>滮出</rb><rt>piao-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>*shü*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>ng-zi-go</rt><rb>男𭑸</rb><rt>nen-nyüing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>讲勒</rb><rt>kông-leh</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>礼拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>礼拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇座</rb><rt>keh-zo</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜个</rb><rt>pa-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>救星</rb><rt>kyiu-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>就是了</rb><rt>ziu-z-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>礼拜</rb><rt>li-pa</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>诚实</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>灵</rb><rt>ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>灵性</rb><rt>ling-sing</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>诚实</rb><rt>dzing-jih</rt><rb>拜个</rb><rt>pa-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>弥赛亚</rb><rt>Mi-sæ-üô</rt><rb>来其</rb><rt>læ-gyi</rt></ruby>。（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。）<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>Kʽeo-kʽeo</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>奇怪</rb><rt>gyi-kwa</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>或者</rb><rt>ʽôh-tsia</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>啥个</rb><rt>Soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>？」<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>放勒</rb><rt>fông-leh</rt><rb>水桶</rb><rt>shü-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>29</sup> 「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道了</rb><rt>dao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>？」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>*Keh-sing*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>Wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>依顺</rb><rt>I-jing</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>完全</rb><rt>wun-djün</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>口粮</rb><rt>kʽeo-liang</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>Tsæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>四个</rb><rt>s-ko</rt><rb>月</rb><rt>yüih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收割个</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>？』<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬带起</rb><rt>dæ-tæn-kyʽi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>田头</rb><rt>din-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>白了</rb><rt>bah-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收割了</rb><rt>siu-keh-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>收割个</rb><rt>Siu-keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>工钿</rb><rt>kong-din</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>收拾</rb><rt>siu-jih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>下秧个</rb><rt>ʽô-iang-go</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>收割个</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>快落</rb><rt>kwʽa-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt><rb>俗话</rb><rt>dzoh-wô</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>下秧个</rb><rt>ʽô-iang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>收割个</rb><rt>siu-keh-go</rt></ruby>。』<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>劳碌</rb><rt>lao-loh</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>*u-dông*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收割</rb><rt>siu-keh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>劳碌过了</rb><rt>lao-loh-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>收</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>劳碌个</rb><rt>lao-loh-go</rt><rb>陶成</rb><rt>*dao-dzing*</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>一切</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道了</rb><rt>dao-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉东</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>庉上</rb><rt>deng-zông</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多了</rb><rt>to-de</rt></ruby>，<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻了</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>救主</rb><rt>Kyiu-cü</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>KO-LEH</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>据</rb><rt>Kyü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>体面</rb><rt>tʽi-min</rt></ruby>。」<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>走</rb><rt>Tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>迎接</rb><rt>nying-tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。

<sup>46</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加利利</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦拿</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>变</rb><rt>pin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>国王</rb><rt>koh-wông</rt><rb>手下</rb><rt>siu-ʽô</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>生病间</rb><rt>sang-bing-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来东了</rb><rt>læ-tong-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>落来</rb><rt>lôh-læ</rt><rb>医</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>儿子个</rb><rt>ng-ts-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>*bing*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>奇事</rb><rt>gyi-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>官</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>tsʽing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>落去</rb><rt>lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>活间了</rb><rt>weh-kæn-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] ʽÔh-wô, ih-go do kwun-fu. 或话，一个官府。（原注）</sup>

<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>落去</rb><rt>Lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>斗上来</rb><rt>teo-zông-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>活了</rb><rt>weh-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>瘥个</rb><rt>tsʽô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>昨日</rb><rt>Zô-nyih</rt><rb>下半日</rb><rt>ʽô-pun-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>热</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>退了</rb><rt>tʽe-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>扣扣</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>活间了</rb><rt>weh-kæn-de</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>和家</rb><rt>ʽo-kô</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>第二个</rb><rt>di-nyi-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. V.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>羊门</rb><rt>Yiang-*meng*</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>池</rb><rt>dzi</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>毕土大</rb><rt>Pih-tʽu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>团圈</rb><rt>*dön-kyʽün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>凉亭</rb><rt>liang-ding</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>里向</rb><rt>Li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>病人</rb><rt>bing-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拐脚</rb><rt>kwa-kyiah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>疯瘫</rb><rt>fong-tʽæn</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>困间</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>单等</rb><rt>tæn-teng</rt><rb>池里个</rb><rt>dzi-li-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>动起来</rb><rt>dong-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>有得</rb><rt>yiu-teh</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>降落</rb><rt>kông-lôh</rt><rb>池里</rb><rt>dzi-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>动</rb><rt>dong</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>动好</rb><rt>dong-hao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>跳落去</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-kyʽi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗论</rb><rt>feh-leng</rt><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>痊愈了</rb><rt>djün-yü-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 31. 耶（稣）后（头）31（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>Keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>病</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>三十</rb><rt>sæn-jih</rt><rb>八</rb><rt>pah</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>困间</rb><rt>kwʽeng-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>病</rb><rt>*bing*</rt><rb>生勒</rb><rt>sang-leh</rt><rb>长久了</rb><rt>dziang-kyiu-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>病人</rb><rt>Bing-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>动个</rb><rt>dong-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>挡</rb><rt>tông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>池里</rb><rt>dzi-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>ngô-zi</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>跳落去</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>铺盖</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>铺盖</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>走了</rb><rt>tseo-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>医好个</rb><rt>i-hao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>今末</rb><rt>*Kyih-mih*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应该</rb><rt>ing-kæ</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>铺盖</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>Do-leh</rt><rb>铺盖</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>Eo</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>铺盖</rb><rt>pʽu-kæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>医好个</rb><rt>I-hao-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一潮</rb><rt>ih-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>暗藏</rb><rt>en-dzông</rt><rb>走过了</rb><rt>tseo-ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, ing-we Yiæ-su, we-leh keh-tʽah di-fông yiu ih-dziao nying læ-tong, z yi-kying bi-ko-de. 有得翻，因为<u>耶稣</u>，为了箇𡍲地方有一潮人来东，是已经避过了。（原注）</sup>

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>碰见</rb><rt>pʽong-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>痊愈了</rb><rt>djün-yü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>遭难</rb><rt>tsao-næn</rt><rb>越发</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>厉害</rb><rt>li-ʽæ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>如今</rb><rt>jü-kying</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>犯着</rb><rt>væn-djôh</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>一样个</rb><rt>ih-yiang-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <sup>24</sup> <sup>30</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>正直</rb><rt>tsing-dzih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>*siang*</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>*i*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>*tæn-tsih*</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一位</rb><rt>Ih-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约翰</rb><rt>Iah-ʽen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>对证过了</rb><rt>te-tsing-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <sup>39</sup>
