<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>尔我个</rb><rt>ng-ngô-go</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>想到</rb><rt>siang-tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>独一位</rb><rt>doh-ih-we</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>状</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>仰望个</rb><rt>nyiang-vông-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

---

### Iʽe. VI.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提比哩亚</rb><rt>Di-pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>病人个</rb><rt>bing-nying-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*Keh-go*</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>Yü-yüih</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>相近了</rb><rt>siang-gying-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] Y. ʽE. 32. 耶（稣）后（头）32（年）。（原注）</sup>

<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一大潮</rb><rt>ih-do-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽAh-li</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>买</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」（<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。）<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>腓力</rb><rt>Fi-lih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>买</rb><rt>*Ma*</rt><rb>廿两</rb><rt>nyiæn-liang</rt><rb>银子</rb><rt>nying-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>各人</rb><rt>kôh-nying</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>够</rb><rt>keo</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><ruby><rb><u>彼得</u>个</rb><rt>Pe-teh-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>安得烈</rb><rt>En-teh-lih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>荡头</rb><rt>Dông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>小人</rb><rt>siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>大麦</rb><rt>do-mah</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>两梗</rb><rt>liang-kwang</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>*ih-ngæn*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>介星</rb><rt>ka-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>Peh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>坐带落</rb><rt>zo-tæn-lôh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt><rb>地</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>男</rb><rt>nen</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>五千</rb><rt>ng-tsʽin</rt><rb>数目</rb><rt>su-moh</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>祝谢仔</rb><rt>coh-zia-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>交付</rb><rt>kao-fu</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>坐间个</rb><rt>zo-kæn-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>*kyʽüoh*</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>吃饱了</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>吃剩个</rb><rt>Kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>收拾拢</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>糟蹋掉</rb><rt>tsao-tʽah-diao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>收拾拢</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>众人</rb><rt>*cong-nying*</rt><rb>吃剩个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-dzing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>五个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>大麦</rb><rt>do-mah</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>零碎</rb><rt>ling-se</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>𣥼满</rb><rt>tsi-mun</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>篮</rb><rt>læn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>当该</rb><rt>tông-kæ</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>硬抲</rb><rt>ngang-kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>独自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>山里</rb><rt>sæn-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>夜快</rb><rt>Yia-kwʽa</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt></ruby>，<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>渡过</rb><rt>du-ko</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>暗了</rb><rt>en-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>大风</rb><rt>do-fong</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>发起来</rb><rt>fah-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>作浪</rb><rt>tsôh-lông</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>摇勒</rb><rt>yiao-leh</rt><rb>十头八里</rb><rt>jih-deo-pah-li</rt><rb>路</rb><rt>lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>面顶</rb><rt>min-teng</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>近拢</rb><rt>gying-long</rt><rb>船里</rb><rt>jün-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>第二</rb><rt>DI-NYI</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>别只</rb><rt>bih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>跳落个</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>落</rb><rt>lôh</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> （<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>几只</rb><rt>kyi-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提比哩亚</rb><rt>Di-pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-leh</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>祝谢好</rb><rt>coh-zia-hao</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。）<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇潮</rb><rt>keh-dziao</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>迦百农</rb><rt>Kyüô-pah-nong</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>对岸</rb><rt>te-ngen</rt><rb>寻着了</rb><rt>zing-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt><rb>到个</rb><rt>tao-go</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>吃饱</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-pao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>饐掉</rb><rt>i-diao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>kong-fu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt><rb>吃食</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-zih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>工夫</rb><rt>*kong-fu*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin*-Vu</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>一颗</rb><rt>ih-kʽo</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>印</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>？」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>Kyʽi</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>旷野</rb><rt>kwʽông-iæ</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>吗嗱</rb><rt>mô-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*Shü-li*</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。』 」<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>*yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>肚饥</rb><rt>du-kyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>永</rb><rt>üong</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>*tso*</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>错落</rb><rt>tsʽo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>Tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>末个</rb><rt>mah-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>爬起来</rb><rt>bô-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落来个</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ-go</rt><rb>饼</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>42</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>约瑟</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-seh-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>？<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>爹娘</rb><rt>tia-nyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>*næn-dao*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>降落来</rb><rt>kông-lôh-læ</rt></ruby>？』」

<sup>43</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>媢怨</rb><rt>mao-ün</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>68</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>69</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>活个</rb><rt>weh-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>70</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>挑选个</rb><rt>tʽiao-shün-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶鬼</rb><rt>ôh-kyü</rt></ruby>。」<sup>71</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加略</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>西门</u>个</rb><rt>Si-meng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>十二个</rb><rt>jih-nyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>在内</rb><rt>dzæ-nen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. VII.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走来走去</rb><rt>tseo-læ-tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>2</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>搭</rb><rt>tah</rt><rb>帐篷</rb><rt>tsiang-bong</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>相近了</rb><rt>siang-gying-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>Gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>Shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>坏个</rb><rt>w̆a-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>未了</rb><rt>mi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>上去</rb><rt>zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>暗洞洞</rb><rt>en-dong-dong</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>节肯</rb><rt>Tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大样</rb><rt>do-nyiang</rt><rb>议论纷纷</rb><rt>nyi-leng-feng-feng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>好人</rb><rt>hao-nying</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>带掉</rb><rt>ta-diao</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>节肯个</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng-go</rt><rb>拗半</rb><rt>ao-pun</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>走上</rb><rt>tseo-zông</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>读过</rb><rt>doh-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, yiu ʽôh-veng. 或话，有学问。（原注）</sup>

<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我自个</rb><rt>Ngô-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>注心</rb><rt>cü-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>想</rb><rt>siang</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>偏曲心</rb><rt>pʽin-kyʽüoh-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>律法</rb><rt>Lih-fah</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>弗然</rb><rt>*feh-jün*</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>入魔的了</rb><rt>jih-mo-tih-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>稀奇</rb><rt>hyi-gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>传</rb><rt>djün</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>*kyʽi-deo*</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>行</rb><rt>ʽang</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>周割礼</rb><rt>tsiu-keh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>违背</rb><rt>we-be</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>浑身</rb><rt>weng-sing</rt><rb>痊愈</rb><rt>djün-yü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>恨</rb><rt>ʽeng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>外光面</rb><rt>nga-kwông-min</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>正直个</rb><rt>tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>Yiæ-lu-sah-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄煞个</rb><rt>long-sah-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>官府</rb><rt>kwun-fu</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>实为</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>？<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>来历</rb><rt>læ-lih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>来历</rb><rt>læ-lih</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>来历</rb><rt>læ-lih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>多如</rb><rt>to-jü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>打发</rb><rt>tang-fah</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>打账</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗着</rb><rt>feh-djôh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>散开</rb><rt>sæn-kʽæ</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><sup> </sup><ruby><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>*pah-sing*</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>*iao*</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>寻弗着</rb><rt>zing-feh-djôh</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>箇是</rb><rt>keh-z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, bih-koh. 或话，别国。（原注）</sup>

<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt><rb>结煞</rb><rt>kyih-sah</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>*nyih-ts*</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>响响</rb><rt>hyiang-hyiang</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>喝</rb><rt>hah</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>Siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>肚皮</rb><rt>du-bi</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>流出</rb><rt>liu-cʽih</rt><rb>活水</rb><rt>weh-shü</rt><rb>河</rb><rt>ʽo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>赐落来</rb><rt>s-*lôh-læ*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>实为</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>Sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>圣书里</rb><rt>Sing-shü-li</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>大辟</u>个</rb><rt>Da-bih-go</rt><rb>子孙</rb><rt>Ts-seng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>伯利恒</rb><rt>Pah-li-ʽeng</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>大辟</rb><rt>Da-bih</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>庉个</rb><rt>deng-go</rt><rb>乡村</rb><rt>hyiang-tsʽeng</rt></ruby>？」<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>百姓</rb><rt>Pah-sing</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>下手</rb><rt>ʽô-siu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>45</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>？」<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>差人</rb><rt>Tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。」<sup>47</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>带掉了</rb><rt>ta-diao-de</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>48</sup> <ruby><rb>官府</rb><rt>Kwun-fu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>49</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>咒骂个</rb><rt>tsiu-mô-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>50</sup> <u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一夜</rb><rt>ih-yia</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>在</rb><rt>dzæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>内</rb><rt>nen</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>51</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>口供</rb><rt>kʽeo-kong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>*sin*</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>52</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>查查看</rb><rt>dzô-dzô-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>各人</rb><rt>Kôh-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>oh-li</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. VIII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>橄榄</rb><rt>Ken-læn</rt><rb>山</rb><rt>sæn</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>早</rb><rt>Tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>众</rb><rt>cong</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>坐落</rb><rt>zo-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>读书人</rb><rt>Doh-shü-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>嫁出个</rb><rt>kô-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>4</sup> 「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>当时</rb><rt>tông-z</rt><rb>抲着个</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>Keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>律法里</rb><rt>lih-fah-li</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞煞个</rb><rt>kʽang-sah-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>试试</rb><rt>s-s</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>因头</rb><rt>*ing-deo*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt></ruby>。

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>伛倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>指头</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>且顾</rb><rt>*tsʽia-kwu*</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>歇口</rb><rt>hyih-kʽeo</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>直起来</rb><rt>dzih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>呒罪个</rb><rt>m-ze-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>一块</rb><rt>ih-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>*kyʽi*</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>伛倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>地垟里</rb><rt>di-yiang-li</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>字</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>被</rb><rt>be</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>良心</rb><rt>liang-sing</rt><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>老老少少</rb><rt>lao-lao-siao-siao</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>走出了</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>剩了</rb><rt>dzing-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>直起来</rb><rt>dzih-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>女人</rb><rt>nyü-nying</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>定</rb><rt>ding</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>犯罪</rb><rt>væn-ze</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>暗路</rb><rt>en-lu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命个</rb><rt>weh-ming-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔自</rb><rt>Ng-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>所以</rb><rt>*sô-yi*</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>Ziu-z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>肉身</rb><rt>nyüoh-sing</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>人家</rb><rt>nying-kô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>独自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>Liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt></ruby>。 <sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt><rb>钱库</rb><rt>dzin-kwʽu</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去仔</rb><rt>kyʽi-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>要防</rb><rt>iao-bông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>下头</rb><rt>ʽô-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>必定</rb><rt>pih-ding</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>*jü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一向</rb><rt>ih-hyiang</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗然</rb><rt>*feh-jün*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>关着</rb><rt>kwæn-djôh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>讲出来</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>提上去</rb><rt>di-zông-kyʽi</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>*jü*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>*we*</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>Tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>独自</rb><rt>doh-zi</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>欢喜个</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>实为</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>；<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>释放了个</rb><rt>sih-fông-liao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>犯罪个</rb><rt>væn-ze-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪个</rb><rt>ze-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只有</rb><rt>tsih-yiu</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>庉东</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>实在</rb><rt>jih-dzæ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>释放了个</rb><rt>sih-fông-liao-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>后代</rb><rt>ʽeo-dæ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心里</rb><rt>sing-li</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒陶成</rb><rt>m-dao-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>Ah-lah-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>儿囡</rb><rt>ng-nô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>亚伯拉罕</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要想</rb><rt>iao-siang</rt><rb>弄煞</rb><rt>long-sah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>听来个</rb><rt>tʽing-læ-go</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>并</rb><rt>bing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>。<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>行为</rb><rt>ʽang-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>奸淫</rb><rt>kæn-ying</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>生个</rb><rt>sang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>42</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>出于</rb><rt>cʽih-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>*tao*</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>*shü-kæn-zông*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt><rb>主意</rb><rt>cü-i</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>43</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>44</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>魔鬼</rb><rt>mo-kwe</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>ah-tia-go</rt><rb>私欲</rb><rt>s-yüôh</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>存</rb><rt>dzeng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>谎话</rb><rt>hwông-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>心想</rb><rt>sing-siang</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>说谎个</rb><rt>shih-hwông-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>说谎</rb><rt>shih-hwông</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>45</sup>
