<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>真话</rb><rt>tsing-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>46</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>靠实</rb><rt>kʽao-jih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>真话</rb><rt>tsing-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<sup>47</sup> <ruby><rb>属于</rb><rt>Joh-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所以</rb><rt>sô-yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>48</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>撒玛利亚</rb><rt>Sah-mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>49</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>入魔个</rb><rt>jih-mo-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敬重</rb><rt>kying-djong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>50</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>*yüong-wô*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>申</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>*Ngô*</rt><rb>冤</rb><rt>ün</rt></ruby>。<sup>51</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>52</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>入了魔了</rb><rt>jih-leh-mo-de</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>*si-de*</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt><rb>苦头</rb><rt>*kwʽu-deo*</rt></ruby>。』<sup>53</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>54</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>算弗得</rb><rt>sön-feh-teh</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>称赞</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>55</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>说谎</rb><rt>shih-hwông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>56</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>祖宗</rb><rt>tsu-tsong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh*</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>日子</rb><rt>nyih-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>高兴</rb><rt>kao-hying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看见过</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢喜了</rb><rt>hwun-hyi-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>57</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>岁</rb><rt>shü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>58</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚伯拉罕</rb><rt>Üô-pah-lah-hen</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>59</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>避过</rb><rt>bi-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是介</rb><rt>ziu-z-ka</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. IX.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*YIÆ-SU*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去个</rb><rt>kyʽi-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>瞎眼个</rb><rt>hah-ngæn-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>夫子</rb><rt>Fu-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>Wa-z</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大人个</rb><rt>do-nying-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>Yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大人个</rb><rt>do-nying-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>现在</rb><rt>*Yin-dzæ*</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>日里</rb><rt>nyih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>弗来</rb><rt>feh-læ</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>快</rb><rt>*kwʽa*</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>亮光</rb><rt>Liang-kwông</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>吐</rb><rt>tʽu</rt><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>馋唾</rb><rt>zæn-tʽu</rt><rb>调</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>瞎子个</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>7</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西罗亚</rb><rt>Si-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>池里</rb><rt>dzi-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt></ruby>。」（<u><ruby><rb>西罗亚</rb><rt>Si-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>解说</rb><rt>ka-shih</rt></ruby>。）<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>转来</rb><rt>cün-læ</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>邻舍家</rb><rt>Ling-sô-kô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>素常</rb><rt>su-djông</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>坐勒</rb><rt>zo-leh</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>讨饭</rb><rt>tʽao-væn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>相貌</rb><rt>Siang-mao</rt><rb>相像</rb><rt>siang-ziang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>开发个</rb><rt>kʽæ-fah-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>调勒</rb><rt>diao-leh</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西罗亚</rb><rt>Si-lo-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>池里</rb><rt>dzi-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>走去</rb><rt>tseo-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>滰</rb><rt>gyiang</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看见了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>调</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>烂泥</rb><rt>na-nyi</rt><rb>搽</rb><rt>djô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>滰滰</rb><rt>gyiang-gyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>看见了</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>守</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>罪人</rb><rt>Ze-nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>既然</rb><rt>kyi-jün</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>先知人</rb><rt>sin-cü-nying</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>18</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>医亮个</rb><rt>i-liang-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>19</sup> 「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>生出</rb><rt>sang-cʽih</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>*ah-lah*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>*yia*</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>*hyiao-teh-go*</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>成人长大</rb><rt>dzing-nying-tsiang-da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>话个</rb><rt>wô-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>约好</rb><rt>iah-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>如果</rb><rt>jü-ko</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>认着</rb><rt>nying-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>赶出</rb><rt>ken-cʽih</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>Keh-lah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大人</rb><rt>do-nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>成人长大</rb><rt>dzing-nying-tsiang-da</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>*Fah-li-sæ*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎眼</rb><rt>hah-ngæn</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>。」<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>罪人</rb><rt>ze-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>做啥个</rb><rt>tso-soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>咋</rb><rt>Dza</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>𧮙骂</rb><rt>zôh-mô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>摩西</u>个</rb><rt>Mo-si-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>摩西</rb><rt>Mo-si</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>稀奇个</rb><rt>hyi-gyi-go</rt></ruby>！<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>罪人个</rb><rt>ze-nying-go</rt><rb>祷告</rb><rt>*tao-kao*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>虔心</rb><rt>gyin-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>旨意</rb><rt>ts-i</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>以来</rb><rt>yi-læ</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>生成</rb><rt>sang-dzing</rt><rb>瞎子个</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>应起</rb><rt>ing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>和然</rb><rt>ʽo-jün</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>罪孽</rb><rt>ze-nyih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>生出来个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>教训</rb><rt>kao-hyüing</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>赶</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>出</rb><rt>cʽih</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ken-cʽih jü-we-dông: Kʽen Iʽe. 10. 或话，赶出聚会堂。看 Iʽe. 10。（原注）</sup>

<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>赶出了</rb><rt>ken-cʽih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>*Jing-ming-go*</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*Ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>*Ze-siu*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拜</rb><rt>pa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>39</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>判断个</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>反转</rb><rt>*fæn-cün*</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>几个</rb><rt>kyi-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>来东个</rb><rt>læ-tong-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>41</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>瞎子</rb><rt>hah-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>看见个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>dzing-gyiu</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。」

### Iʽe. X.

「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>羊圈里</rb><rt>yiang-gyin-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>别处</rb><rt>bih-cʽü</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>跳进去</rb><rt>tʽiao-tsing-kyʽi</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>贼人</rb><rt>zeh-nying</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>羊个</rb><rt>yiang-go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>Meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>先如</rb><rt>sin-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>贼人</rb><rt>zeh-nying</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>Meng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>走进个</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>救</rb><rt>kyiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>进进出出</rb><rt>tsing-tsing-cʽih-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>青草</rb><rt>tsʽing-tsʽao</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>贼</rb><rt>Zeh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>单只</rb><rt>tæn-tsih</rt><rb>偷盗</rb><rt>tʽeo-dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>杀戮</rb><rt>sah-loh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>抄灭</rb><rt>tsʽao-mih</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>*yiang*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>活上</rb><rt>weh-zông</rt><rb>加</rb><rt>kô</rt><rb>活</rb><rt>weh</rt></ruby>。

<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>看羊</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>其自个</rb><rt>gyi-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>雇来个</rb><rt>kwu-læ-go</rt><rb>佣人</rb><rt>yüong-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逃走</rb><rt>dao-tseo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>豺狼</rb><rt>za-lông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽa</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>和潮</rb><rt>ʽo-dziao</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>赶散</rb><rt>ken-sæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>顶</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>看羊个</rb><rt>kʽen-yiang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>*yiang*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>*yiang*</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>替</rb><rt>tʽi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>舍出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我自</rb><rt>Ngô-zi</rt><rb>舍出个</rb><rt>sô-cʽih-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>舍出</rb><rt>sô-cʽih</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>复</rb><rt>voh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>转来个</rb><rt>cün-læ-go</rt><rb>能干</rb><rt>neng-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>接着个</rb><rt>tsih-djôh-go</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>We-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>争论</rb><rt>tsang-leng</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>内中</rb><rt>Nen-cong</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的了</rb><rt>vu-tih-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>发癫个</rb><rt>fah-tin-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>？」<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>有得个</rb><rt>Yiu-teh-go</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>附的</rb><rt>vu-tih</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>鬼</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>开</rb><rt>kʽæ</rt><rb>瞎子个</rb><rt>hah-ts-go</rt><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶路撒冷</rb><rt>YIÆ-LU-SAH-LENG</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>修殿</rb><rt>Siu-din</rt><rb>节肯</rb><rt>tsih-kʽeng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>冬天</rb><rt>tong-tʽin</rt></ruby>，<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>圣殿</rb><rt>sing-din</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>所罗门</u>个</rb><rt>Sô-lo-meng-go</rt><rb>廊屋里</rb><rt>lông-oh-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>围拢来</rb><rt>yü-long-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>弄勒</rb><rt>long-leh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>心挂两肠</rb><rt>sing-kwô-liang-dziang</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>几时</rb><rt>kyi-z</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>声音</rb><rt>sing-ing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>认得</rb><rt>nying-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>灭掉</rb><rt>mih-diao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>*yiang*</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>手里</rb><rt>siu-li</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>夺勒去</rb><rt>deh-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>揞</rb><rt>kʽang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>亵渎个</rb><rt>sih-doh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>*shü-li*</rt><rb>岂</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>jing-ming</rt></ruby>？』 <ruby><rb>何况</rb><rt>*ʽo-hwông*</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>尊起</rb><rt>tseng-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>亵渎</rb><rt>sih-doh</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？<sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>38</sup>
