<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>加略</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-liah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>We-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>庉东</rb><rt>deng-tong</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>我自个</rb><rt>Ngô-zi-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>保家</rb><rt>Pao-kô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>在于</rb><rt>dzæ-ü</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>教</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>记得到</rb><rt>kyi-teh-tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>27</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>遗落</rb><rt>yi-lôh</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>安耽</rb><rt>en-tæn</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>难过</rb><rt>næn-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好甮</rb><rt>hao-vong</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby>。<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>Tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt><rb>之后</rb><rt>ts-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。

<sup>30</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>呒多少</rb><rt>m-to-siao</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>cü-tsæ</rt><rb>来了</rb><rt>læ-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呒关涉</rb><rt>m-kwæn-dzih</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>*z-ken*</rt><rb>却勒</rb><rt>*kyʽiah-leh*</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>爬起</rb><rt>Bô-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>荡头</rb><rt>dông-deo</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」

### Iʽe. XV.

「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真个</rb><rt>tsing-go</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>；<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>种树个</rb><rt>cong-jü-go</rt><rb>师父</rb><rt>S-vu</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>斫掉</rb><rt>tsôh-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>*ô-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>周捉</rb><rt>tsiu-coh</rt><rb>干净</rb><rt>ken-zing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>干净了</rb><rt>ken-zing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>一吊</rb><rt>ih-tiao</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>树个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>葡萄</rb><rt>bu-dao</rt><rb>树</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>*z-go*</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>Væn-pah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>能够</rb><rt>neng-keo</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>桠枝</rb><rt>ô-ts</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>揞勒</rb><rt>kʽang-leh</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>燥掉</rb><rt>sao-diao</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>收拾拢</rb><rt>siu-jih-long</rt><rb>丢勒</rb><rt>tiu-leh</rt><rb>火里</rb><rt>ho-li</rt><rb>𤈦掉</rb><rt>me-diao</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>Ziah-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>做成</rb><rt>tso-dzing</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, ziah-z teng Ngô kah-kʽæ. 或话，若是等我隔开。（原注）</sup>

<sup>9</sup> 「<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>Tsing-ziang</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹个</rb><rt>Ah-tia-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>满足</rb><rt>mun-tsoh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相爱</rb><rt>siang-æ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>像</rb><rt>ziang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>号令</rb><rt>ʽao-ling</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>性命</rb><rt>sing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>呒有</rb><rt>m-yiu</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>箇株介</rb><rt>keh-cü-ka</rt><rb>爱惜个</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>依</rb><rt>i</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>以后</rb><rt>yi-ʽeo</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>朋友</rb><rt>beng-yiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>Feh-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>拣选</rb><rt>kæn-shün</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>设立</rb><rt>shih-lih</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>结</rb><rt>kyih</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>果子</rb><rt>ko-ts</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>靠着</rb><rt>kʽao-djôh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>随便</rb><rt>ze-bin</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>吩咐</rb><rt>feng-fu</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>相爱</rb><rt>siang-æ</rt></ruby>。

<sup>18</sup> 「<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>SHÜ-KÆN-ZÔNG</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>拣选出来个</rb><rt>kæn-shün-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>Nu-boh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>大如</rb><rt>do-jü</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>主人家</rb><rt>cü-nying-kô</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>逼难</rb><rt>pih-næn</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>保守</rb><rt>pao-siu</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>*shih-wô*</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>一切个</rb><rt>ih-tsʽih-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>22</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>推托</rb><rt>tʽe-tʽôh</rt></ruby>。<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>U-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>做过个</rb><rt>tso-ko-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪名</rb><rt>ze-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>Tsih-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>*ziu-z*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>无缘无故</rb><rt>vu-yün-vu-kwu</rt><rb>恶愫</rb><rt>u-su</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』

<sup>26</sup> 「<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差来</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>保家</rb><rt>Pao-kô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理个</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>起头</rb><rt>kyʽi-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。」

### Iʽe. XVI.

「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>𢲔跌</rb><rt>pæn-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt><rb>相貌</rb><rt>siang-mao</rt><rb>害</rb><rt>ʽæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>记得</rb><rt>kyi-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>5</sup> 「<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>Næn-kæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>Cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>？<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>满心</rb><rt>mun-sing</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>实惠</rb><rt>jih-we</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>益处</rb><rt>ih-cʽü</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>离开</rb><rt>li-kʽæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>*ng-lah*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>保家</rb><rt>Pao-kô</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>Cü-kwu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>审判个</rb><rt>sing-pʽun-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>责罚</rb><rt>tsah-vah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>公义</rb><rt>kong-yi</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<sup>11</sup> <ruby><rb>审判</rb><rt>sing-pʽun</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>世间上个</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông-go</rt><rb>主宰</rb><rt>cü-tsæ</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>判断了</rb><rt>pʽun-tön-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, peh shü-kæn-zông hyiao-teh. 或话，拨世间上晓得。（原注）</sup>

<sup>12</sup> 「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>NGÔ</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只是</rb><rt>tsih-z</rt><rb>现在</rb><rt>yin-dzæ</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>吃弗落个</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-feh-lôh-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理个</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>*Sing*-Ling</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>领</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>未来个</rb><rt>vi-læ-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>驮来</rb><rt>do-læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>有星</rb><rt>yiu-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』——<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>？」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>庉</rb><rt>Deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再</rb><rt>tsæ</rt><rb>庉</rb><rt>deng</rt><rb>一晌</rb><rt>ih-zông</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，』<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>议论</rb><rt>nyi-leng</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<sup>20</sup> ### Iʽe. XVII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>眼睛</rb><rt>ngæn-tsing</rt><rb>抬起</rb><rt>dæ-kyʽi</rt><rb>向着</rb><rt>hyiang-djôh</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：

「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>到了</rb><rt>tao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>Ing-we</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血气</rb><rt>hyüih-kyʽi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*Ng-ts*</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>赐</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>永远</rb><rt>üong-yün</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>箇位</rb><rt>keh-we</rt><rb>独一</rb><rt>doh-ih</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>差来个</rb><rt>tsʽa-læ-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u> <u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>完全了</rb><rt>wun-djün-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>受</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>弗曾</rb><rt>feh-zing</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>有个</rb><rt>yiu-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>。

<sup>6</sup>
