<sup>24</sup> 「<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来间个</rb><rt>læ-kæn-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>开辟</rb><rt>kʽæ-bih</rt><rb>天地</rb><rt>tʽin-di</rt><rb>以前</rb><rt>yi-zin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>正直个</rb><rt>Tsing-dzih-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>显出</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>显出来</rb><rt>hyin-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>爱惜</rb><rt>æ-sih</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>心</rb><rt>sing</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其拉个</rb><rt>gyi-lah-go</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>。」

---

### Iʽe. XVIII.

<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>过</rb><rt>ko</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>汲沦</rb><rt>Kyih-leng</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>溪坑</rb><rt>kyʽi-kʽang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>花园</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>Iao</rt><rb>卖</rb><rt>ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉</rb><rt>diao</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>箇𡍲</rb><rt>keh-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>不常</rb><rt>peh-djông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>聚拢</rb><rt>jü-long</rt></ruby>。

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>一队</rb><rt>ih-de</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>法利赛</rb><rt>Fah-li-sæ</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>灯笼</rb><rt>teng-long</rt><rb>火把</rb><rt>ho-pô</rt><rb>刀枪</rb><rt>tao-tsʽiang</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>临</rb><rt>ling</rt><rb>着</rb><rt>djôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>个</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>卖</rb><rt>Ma</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>掉个</rb><rt>diao-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹大</rb><rt>Yiu-da</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>倒退</rb><rt>tao-tʽe</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跌倒</rb><rt>tih-tao</rt><rb>地垟</rb><rt>di-yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>个</rb><rt>Nô-sah-leh-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>话过</rb><rt>wô-ko</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>*læ*</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>Z-ka</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>前头</rb><rt>*zin-deo*</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>失掉</rb><rt>shih-diao</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一把</rb><rt>ih-pô</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>带的</rb><rt>ta-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拔出</rb><rt>bah-cʽih</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>斩</rb><rt>tsæn</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>顺只</rb><rt>jing-tsah</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>奴仆个</rb><rt>nu-boh-go</rt><rb>名字</rb><rt>ming-z</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马勒古</rb><rt>Mô-leh-kwu</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>刀</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>套进</rb><rt>tʽao-tsing</rt><rb>刀壳里</rb><rt>tao-kʽôh-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>杯子</rb><rt>pe-ts</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>难道</rb><rt>næn-dao</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>箇队</rb><rt>keh-de</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>千总</rb><rt>tsʽin-tsong</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>缚仔</rb><rt>bo-ts</rt></ruby>，<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>亚拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>年</rb><rt>nyin</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该亚法</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah-go</rt><rb>丈人</rb><rt>dziang-nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>14</sup> <u><ruby><rb>该亚法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>Ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>百姓</rb><rt>pah-sing</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>阿拉个</rb><rt>*ah-lah-go*</rt><rb>便宜</rb><rt>bin-i</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>跟勒</rb><rt>keng-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>认得个</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>衙门里</rb><rt>ngô-meng-li</rt></ruby>。<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>认得个</rb><rt>nying-teh-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>管门个</rb><rt>kwun-meng-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>*ô-deo*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>进去</rb><rt>tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>管门个</rb><rt>Kwun-meng-go</rt><rb>丫头</rb><rt>ô-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人个</rb><rt>Nying-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。」<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>天家</rb><rt>tʽin-kô</rt><rb>冷</rb><rt>lang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>生</rb><rt>sang</rt><rb>炭火</rb><rt>tʽæn-ho</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>烘</rb><rt>hong</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>烘烘</rb><rt>hong-hong</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>Tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>问问</rb><rt>meng-meng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>明当当</rb><rt>ming-tông-tông</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>长通</rb><rt>dziang-tʽong</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>聚会堂</rb><rt>jü-we-dông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>圣殿里</rb><rt>sing-din-li</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>常常</rb><rt>djông-djông</rt><rb>聚拢个</rb><rt>jü-long-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>乌荡</rb><rt>u-dông</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>一样</rb><rt>ih-yiang</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>讲过</rb><rt>kông-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>听闻个</rb><rt>tʽing-meng-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>啥西</rb><rt>soh-si</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>正好</rb><rt>tsing-hao</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立东个</rb><rt>lih-tong-go</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>掴</rb><rt>kwah</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一光</rb><rt>ih-kwông</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>应</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>23</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>讲错了</rb><rt>kông-tsʽo-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>批评</rb><rt>pʽi-bing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>错</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>错</rb><rt>tsʽo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>咋会</rb><rt>dza-we</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, s bông tang Yiæ-su ih-kyi. 或话，使棒打<u>耶稣</u>一记。（原注）</sup>

<sup>24</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>缚了个</rb><rt>bo-liao-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>亚拿</rb><rt>Üô-nô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>送去</rb><rt>song-kyʽi</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该亚法</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah</rt></ruby></u>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立仔</rb><rt>lih-ts</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>烘火</rb><rt>hong-ho</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>祭司头个</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo-go</rt><rb>奴仆</rb><rt>nu-boh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>削落</rb><rt>siah-lôh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>耳朵</rb><rt>ng-to</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>亲眷</rb><rt>tsʽing-kyün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>花园</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>27</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>招认</rb><rt>tsiao-jing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>鸡</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>立刻</rb><rt>lih-kʽeh</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>啼了</rb><rt>di-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>KEH-TSAO</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该亚法</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-üô-fah-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>公馆里</rb><rt>kong-kwun-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>Z-ʽeo</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>早</rb><rt>tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>恐怕</rb><rt>kʽong-pʽô</rt><rb>黵着</rb><rt>tæn-djôh</rt><rb>鏖糟</rb><rt>ao-tsao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>公馆里</rb><rt>kong-kwun-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>逾越</rb><rt>yü-yüih</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh* *wô*, Pe-lah-to ngô-meng: *kʽen* Mt. 27. 27. 或话，<u>彼拉多</u>衙门。看 Mt. 27. 27.（原注）</sup>

<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>告</rb><rt>kao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>案件</rb><rt>en-gyin</rt></ruby>？」<sup>30</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>坏</rb><rt>w̆a</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>再𣍐</rb><rt>tsæ-ve</rt><rb>送来</rb><rt>song-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。」<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>带勒去</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>判断</rb><rt>pʽun-tön</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>杀人个</rb><rt>sah-nying-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt></ruby>。」<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>S-teh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>死法</rb><rt>si-fah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>。

<sup>33</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>公馆里</rb><rt>kong-kwun-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>讴</rb><rt>eo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>34</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>Keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔自</rb><rt>ng-zi</rt><rb>讲出来个</rb><rt>kông-cʽih-læ-go</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>还是</rb><rt>wa-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道个</rb><rt>dao-go</rt></ruby>？」<sup>35</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>啥</rb><rt>soh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>Z</rt><rb>尔自个</rb><rt>Ng-zi-go</rt><rb>本国人</rb><rt>peng-koh-nying</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>送来</rb><rt>song-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>？」<sup>36</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>手下人</rb><rt>siu-ʽô-nying</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>出力</rb><rt>cʽih-lih</rt><rb>打仗</rb><rt>tang-tsiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>næn-kæn</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>国</rb><rt>koh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>37</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>Ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>话着了</rb><rt>wô-djôh-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>生出来个</rb><rt>sang-cʽih-læ-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理个</rb><rt>dao-li-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>属于</rb><rt>joh-ü</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>听</rb><rt>tʽing</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>。」<sup>38</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>啥西</rb><rt>Soh-si</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真</rb><rt>tsing</rt><rb>道理</rb><rt>dao-li</rt></ruby>？」

<ruby><rb>话勒</rb><rt>Wô-leh</rt><rb>一声</rb><rt>ih-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出去</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>犯人</rb><rt>væn-nying</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>介呢</rb><rt>ka-ni</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>fông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>众人</rb><rt>Cong-nying</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>Feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>放</rb><rt>*fông*</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>放</rb><rt>*fông*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u>。」<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>巴拉巴</rb><rt>Pô-lah-pô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>强盗</rb><rt>gyiang-dao</rt></ruby>。

---

### Iʽe. XIX.

<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>使</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>鞭子</rb><rt>pin-ts</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>编出</rb><rt>pin-cʽih</rt><rb>一顶</rb><rt>ih-ting</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戴</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>头里</rb><rt>deo-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>红袍</rb><rt>ʽong-bao</rt><rb>披勒</rb><rt>pʽi-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>身上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<sup>3</sup> 「<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>恭喜</rb><rt>kong-hyi</rt></ruby>！」<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>驮起</rb><rt>do-kyʽi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>打</rb><rt>tang</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>带</rb><rt>ta</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>刺</rb><rt>tsʽ</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>平天冠</rb><rt>bing-tʽin-kwun</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>披勒</rb><rt>pʽi-leh</rt><rb>红袍</rb><rt>ʽong-bao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>走出来</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>*Pe-lah-to*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>！」<sup>6</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>差人</rb><rt>tsʽa-nying</rt><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>Ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>！」<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>。」<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一条</rb><rt>ih-diao</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>律法</rb><rt>lih-fah</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>该</rb><rt>kæ</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>算</rb><rt>sön</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>Zi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>8</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>越发</rb><rt>yüih-fah</rt><rb>怕起来了</rb><rt>pʽô-kyʽi-læ-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>官厅里</rb><rt>kwun-tʽing-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>到底</rb><rt>tao-ti</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>应</rb><rt>ing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>肯</rb><rt>kʽeng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>难道</rb><rt>Næn-dao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>Ziah</rt><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>赐拨</rb><rt>s-peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>权柄</rb><rt>gyün-ping</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>办</rb><rt>bæn</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>送</rb><rt>song</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>愈加</rb><rt>yü-kô</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>打算</rb><rt>tang-sön</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>Dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>释放</rb><rt>sih-fông</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>Nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗是</rb><rt>feh-z</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>该撒</u>个</rb><rt>Kæ-sah-go</rt><rb>忠臣</rb><rt>cong-dzing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>凡百</rb><rt>væn-pah</rt><rb>自立为王</rb><rt>zi-lih-we-wông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>得罪</rb><rt>teh-ze</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u>。」<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>出来</rb><rt>cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>座位里</rb><rt>zo-we-li</rt><rb>坐仔</rb><rt>zo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>铺华石</rb><rt>Pʽu-wô-zah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>希伯来</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>*kyiao-leh*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>厄巴大</rb><rt>Eh-pô-da</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>14</sup> （<ruby><rb>时候</rb><rt>*z-ʽeo*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>预备</rb><rt>yü-be</rt><rb>逾越节</rb><rt>Yü-yüih-tsih</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>约来</rb><rt>iah-læ</rt><rb>十二</rb><rt>jih-nyi</rt><rb>点钟</rb><rt>tin-cong</rt></ruby><sup> </sup>。）<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>！」<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>怀怀响</rb><rt>wæ-wæ-hyiang</rt><rb>讴起来</rb><rt>eo-kyʽi-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>Kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>弃掉</rb><rt>kyʽi-diao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>*Gyi*</rt></ruby>！<ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>Ng-lah-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>除了</rb><rt>Djü-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>该撒</rb><rt>Kæ-sah</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>之外</rb><rt>ts-nga</rt><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>wông-ti</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>*Pe-lah-to*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将</rb><rt>tsiang</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *Yiu-teh* *fæn*, loh tin-cong. *Yiu-kyi-go* *kwu-kyün* *wô*, kyiu tin-cong. 有得翻，六点钟。有几个古卷话，九点钟。（原注）</sup>

<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>GYI-LAH</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>带勒去了</rb><rt>ta-leh-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>揹勒</rb><rt>pe-leh</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt><rb>骷颅头</rb><rt>Kwʽu-lu-deo</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>希伯来</u>个</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ-go</rt><rb>口音</rb><rt>kʽeo-ing</rt><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>各各他</rb><rt>Kôh-kôh-tʽa</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>钉间</rb><rt>*ting-kæn*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>荡边</rb><rt>dông-pin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>*ih-go*</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇边</rb><rt>keh-pin</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>一张</rb><rt>ih-tsiang</rt><rb>条子</rb><rt>diao-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>贴</rb><rt>tʽiah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>写的个</rb><rt>sia-tih-go</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>拿撒勒</u>个</rb><rt>NÔ-SAH-LEH-GO</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>YIÆ-SU</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>YIU-TʽA</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>NYING-GO</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>WÔNG-TI</rt></ruby>。」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>读</rb><rt>doh</rt><rb>箇张</rb><rt>keh-tsiang</rt><rb>条子</rb><rt>diao-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>城里</rb><rt>zing-li</rt><rb>相近</rb><rt>siang-gying</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>希伯来</rb><rt>Hyi-pah-læ</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>希腊</rb><rt>Hyi-lah</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb><u>罗马</u>个</rb><rt>Lo-mô-go</rt><rb>字眼</rb><rt>z-ngæn</rt><rb>写个</rb><rt>sia-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>祭司头</rb><rt>tsi-s-deo</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>甮</rb><rt>Vong</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby>，『<u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>。』<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>Gyi-zi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>皇帝</rb><rt>Wông-ti</rt></ruby>。』」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>写的了</rb><rt>sia-tih-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>既经</rb><rt>kyi-kying</rt><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt><rb>四股</rb><rt>s-kwu</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>每人</rb><rt>me-nying</rt><rb>各</rb><rt>kôh</rt><rb>得</rb><rt>teh</rt><rb>一股</rb><rt>ih-kwu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>*do-leh*</rt><rb>替里</rb><rt>tʽi-li</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>替里</rb><rt>tʽi-li</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>缝乌</rb><rt>vong-u</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>做出</rb><rt>tso-cʽih</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>一件生</rb><rt>ih-gyin-sang</rt></ruby>。<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>甮</rb><rt>vong</rt><rb>扯开</rb><rt>cʽô-kʽæ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇节</rb><rt>keh-tsih</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>外头</rb><rt>nga-deo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>分开</rb><rt>feng-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>里头</rb><rt>li-deo</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>撮</rb><rt>tsʽeh</rt><rb>指末头</rb><rt>ts-meh-den</rt></ruby>。」 <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘个</rb><rt>ah-nyiang-go</rt><rb>姊妹</rb><rt>tsi-me</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>革罗巴</u><sup> </sup>个</rb><rt>Keh-lo-pô-go</rt><rb>老𭑸</rb><rt>lao-nyüing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立间</rb><rt>lih-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>26</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>爱惜个</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>旁边</rb><rt>bông-pin</rt><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，」<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby>。」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>Yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>且看</rb><rt>Tsʽia-kʽen</rt><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>阿娘</rb><rt>ah-nyiang</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>Dzong</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>接</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>屋里</rb><rt>*oh-li*</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>\[1\] *ʽÔh-tsia*, Keh-liu-pô-go. 或者，<u>革流巴</u>个。（原注）</sup>

<sup>28</sup> <ruby><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽEo-deo</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>完全了</rb><rt>wun-djün-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>口渴</rb><rt>kʽeo-kʽeh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>29</sup> <ruby><rb>箇头</rb><rt>*Keh-deo*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>满满</rb><rt>mun-mun</rt><rb>一瓶</rb><rt>ih-bing</rt><rb>酸酒</rb><rt>sön-tsiu</rt><rb>安间</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>海绵花</rb><rt>hæ-min-hwô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>酒</rb><rt>tsiu</rt><rb>渗透仔</rb><rt>seng-tʽeo-ts</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>戳</rb><rt>cʽoh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>牛膝</rb><rt>ngeo-sih</rt><rb>草里</rb><rt>tsʽao-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>递</rb><rt>di</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>嘴巴</rb><rt>cü-pô</rt></ruby>。<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶</rb><rt>Yiæ</rt><rb>稣</rb><rt>su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>受勒</rb><rt>ziu-leh</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>酸酒</rb><rt>sön-tsiu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>事干</rb><rt>Z-ken</rt><rb>完全了</rb><rt>wun-djün-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>keh-tsao</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>伛倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>Zi-go</rt><rb>活灵</rb><rt>weh-ling</rt><rb>交代</rb><rt>kao-dæ</rt><rb>天父</rb><rt>*Tʽin-Vu*</rt></ruby>。

<sup>31</sup> <u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>*keh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>预备日</rb><rt>yü-be-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>省勒</rb><rt>sæn-leh</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt><rb>挂</rb><rt>kwô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>十字架上</rb><rt>jih-z-kô-zông</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt><rb>大</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>安息日</rb><rt>en-sih-nyih</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>应许</rb><rt>ing-hyü</rt><rb>敲断</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>脚骨</rb><rt>kyiah-kweh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮过</rb><rt>do-ko</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>*s-siu*</rt></ruby>。<sup>32</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>敲断</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>同队</rb><rt>dong-de</rt><rb>钉间</rb><rt>ting-kæn</rt><rb>主顾个</rb><rt>cü-kwu-go</rt><rb>脚骨</rb><rt>kyiah-kweh</rt></ruby>。<sup>33</sup> <ruby><rb>到了</rb><rt>Tao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>死了</rb><rt>si-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>敲断</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>脚骨</rb><rt>kyiah-kweh</rt></ruby>。<sup>34</sup> <ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>兵</rb><rt>ping</rt><rb>驮勒</rb><rt>do-leh</rt><rb>一支</rb><rt>ih-ts</rt><rb>枪</rb><rt>tsʽiang</rt><rb>戳进</rb><rt>cʽoh-tsing</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>腰胁里</rb><rt>iao-hyih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>血</rb><rt>hyüih</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>水</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>流出来</rb><rt>liu-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>35</sup> <ruby><rb>做</rb><rt>Tso</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>亲眼</rb><rt>*tsʽing-ngæn*</rt><rb>看见过个</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin-ko-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其自</rb><rt>gyi-zi</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>讲个</rb><rt>kông-go</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>真话</rb><rt>tsing-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<sup>36</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>做法</rb><rt>tso-fah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>使得</rb><rt>s-teh</rt><rb>箇节</rb><rt>keh-tsih</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>效验</rb><rt>yiao-nyiæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>骨头</rb><rt>kweh-deo</rt><rb>一根</rb><rt>ih-keng</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>敲断</rb><rt>kʽao-dön</rt></ruby>。」 <sup>37</sup> <ruby><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>一节</rb><rt>ih-tsih</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>戳伤</rb><rt>cʽoh-sông</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>。」 

<sup>38</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>亚利马太</u>个</rb><rt>Üô-li-mô-tʽa-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>约瑟</rb><rt>Iah-seh</rt></ruby></u>，（<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>做</rb><rt>tso</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>不过</rb><rt>peh-ko</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>私下</rb><rt>s-ʽô</rt><rb>做个</rb><rt>tso-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>*keh-go*</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>求</rb><rt>gyiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>彼拉多</rb><rt>Pe-lah-to</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>应许了</rb><rt>ing-hyü-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>驮勒去</rb><rt>do-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>39</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>Wa-yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>尼哥底母</rb><rt>Nyi-ko-ti-meo</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>前头</rb><rt>zin-deo</rt><rb>夜到</rb><rt>yia-tao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>见</rb><rt>kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>带勒</rb><rt>ta-leh</rt><rb>靠百斤</rb><rt>kʽao-pah-kying</rt><rb>没药</rb><rt>meh-yiah</rt><rb>和勒</rb><rt>ʽo-leh</rt><rb>芦荟</rb><rt>lu-we</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>40</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>照</rb><rt>tsiao</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>葬个</rb><rt>tsông-go</rt><rb>规矩</rb><rt>kwe-kyü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>用</rb><rt>yüong</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>安勒</rb><rt>en-leh</rt><rb>香料</rb><rt>hyiang-liao</rt><rb>裹带拢</rb><rt>ko-tæn-long</rt></ruby>。

<sup>41</sup> <ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>钉</rb><rt>ting</rt><rb>十字架</rb><rt>jih-z-kô</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>花园</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>花园</rb><rt>hwô-yün</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一穴</rb><rt>ih-yüih</rt><rb>寿坟</rb><rt>ziu-veng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>葬过</rb><rt>tsông-ko</rt></ruby>。<sup>42</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>*Keh*</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>*nyih*</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人个</rb><rt>nying-go</rt><rb>预备日</rb><rt>yü-be-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>再话</rb><rt>tsæ-wô</rt><rb>箇穴</rb><rt>keh-yüih</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>近便</rb><rt>gying-bin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇头</rb><rt>keh-deo</rt><rb>葬</rb><rt>tsông</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。

### Iʽe. XX.

<ruby><rb>七</rb><rt>TSʽIH</rt><rb>日个</rb><rt>NYIH-GO</rt><rb>头日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>黑早</rb><rt>heh-tsao</rt><rb>天亮</rb><rt>tʽin-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>石头</rb><rt>zah-deo</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>移过了</rb><rt>yi-ko-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>奔去</rb><rt>peng-kyʽi</rt><rb>寻着</rb><rt>zing-djôh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>爱惜个</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>*s-siu*</rt><rb>坟里</rb><rt>veng-li</rt><rb>驮出</rb><rt>do-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>了</rb><rt>de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>Liang-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>平齐</rb><rt>bing-zi</rt><rb>奔勒去</rb><rt>peng-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>奔勒</rb><rt>peng-leh</rt><rb>快</rb><rt>kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt></ruby>。<sup>5</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>伛倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>安间</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>随后</rb><rt>ze-ʽeo</rt><rb>赶到</rb><rt>ken-tao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走进</rb><rt>tseo-tsing</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>安间</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>箇块</rb><rt>keh-kwʽe</rt><rb>包</rb><rt>pao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>头</rb><rt>deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>手巾</rb><rt>siu-kying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>麻布</rb><rt>mô-pu</rt><rb>做𡍲</rb><rt>tso-tʽah</rt><rb>安间</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>另外</rb><rt>ling-nga</rt><rb>一𡍲</rb><rt>ih-tʽah</rt><rb>折拢间</rb><rt>tsih-long-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>别个</rb><rt>bih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>先</rb><rt>sin</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>坟头</rb><rt>veng-deo</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>走进去</rb><rt>tseo-tsing-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>相信了</rb><rt>siang-sing-de</rt></ruby>。<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>明白</rb><rt>ming-bah</rt><rb>圣书</rb><rt>Sing-shü</rt><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>必</rb><rt>pih</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>*liang*</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kyü</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。

<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>仍旧</rb><rt>*dzing-gyiu*</rt><rb>立</rb><rt>lih</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>门口</rb><rt>meng-kʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>哭个</rb><rt>Kʽoh-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>朝勒</rb><rt>dziao-leh</rt><rb>坟</rb><rt>veng</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>伛倒</rb><rt>eo-tao</rt><rb>看看</rb><rt>kʽen-kʽen</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>两</rb><rt>liang</rt><rb>天使</rb><rt>tʽin-s</rt><rb>穿</rb><rt>cʽün</rt><rb>白白个</rb><rt>bah-bah-go</rt><rb>衣裳</rb><rt>i-zông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>坐间</rb><rt>zo-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>头沿</rb><rt>deo-yin</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>脚沿</rb><rt>kyiah-yin</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <ruby><rb>天使</rb><rt>Tʽin-s</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>主个</rb><rt>Cü-go</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>s-siu</rt><rb>驮勒去</rb><rt>do-leh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>弗照</rb><rt>feh-tsiao</rt><rb>安</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt></ruby>。」<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立东</rb><rt>lih-tong</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>15</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>女人</rb><rt>Nyü-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>哭</rb><rt>kʽoh</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt><rb>寻</rb><rt>zing</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>道是</rb><rt>dao-z</rt><rb>管园个</rb><rt>kwun-yün-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>Sin-sang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>把</rb><rt>pô</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>尸首</rb><rt>*s-siu*</rt><rb>驮过了</rb><rt>do-ko-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>安间</rb><rt>en-kæn</rt><rb>话话</rb><rt>wô-wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u>！」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>拉波尼</rb><rt>Lah-po-nyi</rt></ruby>！」（<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt></ruby>「<ruby><rb>先生</rb><rt>sin-sang</rt></ruby>」<ruby><rb>意思</rb><rt>i-s</rt></ruby>。）<sup>17</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>M-nao</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>按</rb><rt>en</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，『<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>尔拉个</rb><rt>ng-lah-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>升上</rb><rt>sing-zông</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。』」

<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb><u>抹大拉</u>个</rb><rt>Meh-da-lah-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>马利亚</rb><rt>Mô-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>通知</rb><rt>tʽong-cü</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>*Cü*</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>讲过了</rb><rt>kông-ko-de</rt></ruby>。

<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>当日</rb><rt>TÔNG-NYIH</rt><rb>夜快</rb><rt>yia-kwʽa</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇</rb><rt>keh</rt><rb>七日个</rb><rt>tsʽih-nyih-go</rt><rb>头日</rb><rt>deo-nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>聚拢间个</rb><rt>jü-long-kæn-go</rt><rb>乌碎</rb><rt>u-sen</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>怕</rb><rt>pʽô</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>犹太</rb><rt>Yiu-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>门</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>关了个</rb><rt>kwæn-liao-go</rt></ruby>。<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>！」<sup>20</sup> <ruby><rb>箇句</rb><rt>Keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>腰胁</rb><rt>iao-hyih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>欢喜</rb><rt>hwun-hyi</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>正像</rb><rt>tsing-ziang</rt><rb>阿爹</rb><rt>Ah-tia</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>难间</rb><rt>*næn-kæn*</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽa</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<sup>22</sup> <ruby><rb>说话</rb><rt>Shih-wô</rt><rb>讲好</rb><rt>kông-hao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>颬</rb><rt>hô</rt><rb>一口</rb><rt>ih-kʽeo</rt><rb>气</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>接受</rb><rt>tsih-ziu</rt><rb>圣灵</rb><rt>Sing-Ling</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>Ng-lah</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>赦掉</rb><rt>sô-diao</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>ze</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>赦掉了</rb><rt>sô-diao-de</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>留住</rb><rt>liu-djü</rt><rb>谁个</rb><rt>jü-go</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>*gyi-lah*</rt><rb>罪</rb><rt>*ze*</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>留住了</rb><rt>liu-djü-de</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>十二</rb><rt>Jih-nyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一个</rb><rt>ih-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>低土马</rb><rt>Ti-tʽu-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>25</sup> <ruby><rb>余多</rb><rt>Yü-to</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>已经</rb><rt>yi-kying</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt><rb>过了</rb><rt>ko-de</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若</rb><rt>ziah</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>手里个</rb><rt>siu-li-go</rt><rb>钉子</rb><rt>ting-cü</rt><rb>乌</rb><rt>u</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>指头</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>钉子</rb><rt>ting-cü</rt><rb>乌</rb><rt>u</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>并且</rb><rt>ping-tsʽia</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>摸着</rb><rt>moh-djôh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>腰胁里</rb><rt>iao-hyih-li</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>26</sup> <ruby><rb>过了</rb><rt>Ko-leh</rt><rb>八</rb><rt>pah</rt><rb>日</rb><rt>nyih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>屋落</rb><rt>oh-lô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门</rb><rt>Meng</rt><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>关了个</rb><rt>kwæn-liao-go</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>立勒</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是介</rb><rt>z-ka</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>请</rb><rt>Tsʽing</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>平安</rb><rt>bing-en</rt></ruby>！」<sup>27</sup> <ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>Ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>Ng-go</rt><rb>指头</rb><rt>ts-deo</rt><rb>伸出来</rb><rt>sing-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔个</rb><rt>ng-go</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt><rb>伸出来</rb><rt>sing-cʽih-læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>摸摸</rb><rt>moh-moh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>腰胁里</rb><rt>iao-hyih-li</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>呒得好</rb><rt>m-nao</rt><rb>是介</rb><rt>*z-ka*</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>总</rb><rt>tsong</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。」<sup>28</sup> <u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我个</rb><rt>ngô-go</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>！」<sup>29</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇拉</rb><rt>keh-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>相信个</rb><rt>siang-sing-go</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>倒</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>福气</rb><rt>foh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>30</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>面前</rb><rt>min-zin</rt><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>神迹</rb><rt>jing-tsih</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>箇本</rb><rt>keh-peng</rt><rb>书里</rb><rt>shü-li</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>。<sup>31</sup> <ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>Keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>写的</rb><rt>sia-tih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>基督</rb><rt>Kyi-toh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>神明个</rb><rt>Jing-ming-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>Ng-ts</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>相信</rb><rt>siang-sing</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>Gyi-go</rt><rb>名头</rb><rt>ming-deo</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>得着</rb><rt>teh-djôh</rt><rb>活命</rb><rt>weh-ming</rt></ruby>。
