### Iʽe. XXI.

<ruby><rb>箇星</rb><rt>KEH-SING</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>提比哩亚</rb><rt>Di-pi-li-üô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>海</rb><rt>hæ</rt><rb>边沿</rb><rt>pin-yin</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>介个</rb><rt>ka-go</rt></ruby>。<sup>2</sup> <ruby><rb>有</rb><rt>Yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>多马</rb><rt>To-mô</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>叫勒</rb><rt>kyiao-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>低土马</rb><rt>Ti-tʽu-mô</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>加利利</rb><rt>Kyüô-li-li</rt></ruby></u> <ruby><rb><u>迦拿</u>个</rb><rt>Kyüô-nô-go</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>拿但业</rb><rt>Nô-dæn-nyih</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西庇太</rb><rt>Si-pi-tʽa</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>两个</rb><rt>*liang-go*</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>两个</rb><rt>liang-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>都</rb><rt>tu</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>3</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>抲</rb><rt>kʽô</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>去了</rb><rt>kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>Ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>大家</rb><rt>dô-kô</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>走出</rb><rt>tseo-cʽih</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>ze-siu</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>一只</rb><rt>ih-tsah</rt><rb>船</rb><rt>jün</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>当夜</rb><rt>tông-yia</rt><rb>一眼</rb><rt>ih-ngæn</rt><rb>呒告</rb><rt>m-kao</rt><rb>抲着</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh</rt></ruby>。

<sup>4</sup> <ruby><rb>已经</rb><rt>Yi-kying</rt><rb>天</rb><rt>tʽin</rt><rb>亮了</rb><rt>liang-de</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>立了</rb><rt>lih-leh</rt><rb>海涂上</rb><rt>hæ-du-zông</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u>。<sup>5</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>小人</rb><rt>Siao-nying</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔拉</rb><rt>ng-lah</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>下饭</rb><rt>ʽô-væn</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>回答</rb><rt>we-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>M̆-teh</rt></ruby>。」<sup>6</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>来</rb><rt>Læ</rt><rb>船个</rb><rt>jün-go</rt><rb>顺手边</rb><rt>jing-siu-pin</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>下</rb><rt>ʽô</rt><rb>网</rb><rt>mông</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>抲着</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>下落去</rb><rt>ʽô-lôh-kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>网</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>拖弗动</rb><rt>tʽo-feh-dong</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>缘故</rb><rt>yün-kwu</rt></ruby>。<sup>7</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>爱惜个</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>一</rb><rt>ih</rt><rb>听闻</rb><rt>tʽing-meng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>穿勒</rb><rt>cʽün-leh</rt><rb>布衫</rb><rt>pu-sæn</rt></ruby>，（<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>出膊个</rb><rt>cʽih-poh-go</rt></ruby>，）<ruby><rb>随手</rb><rt>*ze-siu*</rt><rb>跳落</rb><rt>tʽiao-lôh</rt><rb>海里</rb><rt>hæ-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<sup>8</sup> <ruby><rb>余多个</rb><rt>Yü-to-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>驶</rb><rt>s</rt><rb>小船</rb><rt>siao-jün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拖勒</rb><rt>tʽo-leh</rt><rb>箇网</rb><rt>keh-mông</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>拢来</rb><rt>long-læ</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>离</rb><rt>li</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>远</rb><rt>yün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>只</rb><rt>tsih</rt><rb>差</rb><rt>tsʽô</rt><rb>廿把</rb><rt>nyiæn-pô</rt><rb>丈</rb><rt>dziang</rt></ruby>。

<sup>9</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>炭火</rb><rt>tʽæn-ho</rt><rb>生间</rb><rt>sang-kæn</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>火</rb><rt>ho</rt><rb>上头</rb><rt>zông-deo</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>来的</rb><rt>læ-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>来间</rb><rt>læ-kæn</rt></ruby>。<sup>10</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>才始</rb><rt>Dzæ-s</rt><rb>抲着个</rb><rt>kʽô-djôh-go</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>几梗</rb><rt>kyi-kwang</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>。」<sup>11</sup> <u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走落</rb><rt>tseo-lôh</rt><rb>箇只</rb><rt>*keh-tsah*</rt><rb>小船里</rb><rt>*siao-jün-li*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拖</rb><rt>tʽo</rt><rb>网</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>上</rb><rt>zông</rt><rb>岸</rb><rt>ngen</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>满网</rb><rt>mun-mông</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>一百</rb><rt>ih-pah</rt><rb>五十</rb><rt>ng-jih</rt><rb>三梗</rb><rt>sæn-kwang</rt><rb>大大</rb><rt>do-do</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>虽即</rb><rt>se-tsih</rt><rb>介</rb><rt>ka</rt><rb>多</rb><rt>to</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>网</rb><rt>mông</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m-neh</rt><rb>裂开</rb><rt>lih-kʽæ</rt></ruby>。<sup>12</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>门徒</rb><rt>Meng-du</rt><rb>呒人</rb><rt>m-nying</rt><rb>敢</rb><rt>ken</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>因为</rb><rt>ing-we</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>。<sup>13</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>来</rb><rt>læ</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>驮</rb><rt>do</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>馒头</rb><rt>mun-deo</rt><rb>分</rb><rt>feng</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其拉</rb><rt>gyi-lah</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>鱼</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>是介个</rb><rt>z-ka-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>14</sup> <ruby><rb>箇</rb><rt>Keh</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>从</rb><rt>dzong</rt><rb>死人</rb><rt>si-nying</rt><rb>中央</rb><rt>cong-nyiang</rt><rb>活转来</rb><rt>weh-cün-læ</rt><rb>后头</rb><rt>ʽeo-deo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>现出来</rb><rt>yin-cʽih-læ</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>看</rb><rt>kʽen</rt></ruby>。

<sup>15</sup> <ruby><rb>其拉</rb><rt>Gyi-lah</rt><rb>吃好</rb><rt>kyʽüoh-hao</rt></ruby>，<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u><u>·</u><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>*ng-ts*</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>比</rb><rt>pi</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>*nying*</rt><rb>更加</rb><rt>keng-kô</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>吗</rb><rt>ma</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喂</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>小羊</rb><rt>siao-yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>16</sup> <ruby><rb>第二遭</rb><rt>Di-nyi-tsao</rt><rb>又</rb><rt>yi</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是个</rb><rt>z-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>管</rb><rt>kwun</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。」<sup>17</sup> <ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>Di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb><u>约拿</u>个</rb><rt>Iah-nô-go</rt><rb>儿子</rb><rt>ng-ts</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>西门</rb><rt>Si-meng</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u>，<ruby><rb>为了</rb><rt>we-leh</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>*Yiæ-su*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>第三遭</rb><rt>di-sæn-tsao</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>忧闷</rb><rt>iu-meng</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>样样</rb><rt>yiang-yiang</rt><rb>晓得个</rb><rt>hyiao-teh-go</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>爱敬</rb><rt>æ-kying</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt></ruby>。」<u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>好</rb><rt>Hao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>喂</rb><rt>ü</rt><rb>我个</rb><rt>Ngô-go</rt><rb>羊</rb><rt>yiang</rt></ruby>。<sup>18</sup> <ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>真真</rb><rt>tsing-tsing</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt><rb>向</rb><rt>hyiang</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>道</rb><rt>dao</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>还</rb><rt>wa</rt><rb>轻</rb><rt>kyʽing</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>自</rb><rt>zi</rt><rb>记</rb><rt>kyi</rt><rb>自个</rb><rt>zi-go</rt><rb>缴身带</rb><rt>*kyiao-sing-ta*</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>走</rb><rt>tseo</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>何里</rb><rt>ʽah-li</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>等到</rb><rt>teng-tao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>年纪</rb><rt>nyin-kyi</rt><rb>老</rb><rt>lao</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>会</rb><rt>we</rt><rb>伸开</rb><rt>sing-kʽæ</rt><rb>手</rb><rt>siu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>别人</rb><rt>bih-nying</rt><rb>缚勒</rb><rt>bo-leh</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>扛</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>弗</rb><rt>feh</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>去</rb><rt>kyʽi</rt><rb>个</rb><rt>go</rt><rb>地方</rb><rt>di-fông</rt></ruby>。」<sup>19</sup> <ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>讲</rb><rt>kông</rt><rb>箇句</rb><rt>keh-kyü</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>指点</rb><rt>ts-tin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>*Pe-teh*</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>将来</rb><rt>tsiang-læ</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>咋个</rb><rt>dza-go</rt><rb>死法</rb><rt>si-fah</rt><rb>归</rb><rt>kwe</rt><rb>荣华</rb><rt>yüong-wô</rt><rb>拨</rb><rt>peh</rt><rb>神明</rb><rt>Jing-ming</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>讲好</rb><rt>Kông-hao</rt><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>好</rb><rt>hao</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>。」

<sup>20</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>扭转</rb><rt>nyin-cün</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>所</rb><rt>sô</rt><rb>爱惜个</rb><rt>æ-sih-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>跟的</rb><rt>keng-tih</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>吃</rb><rt>kyʽüoh</rt><rb>夜饭</rb><rt>yia-væn</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt><rb>戤到</rb><rt>gæ-tao</rt></ruby><ruby><rb><u>耶稣</u>个</rb><rt>Yiæ-su-go</rt><rb>胸脘头</rb><rt>hyüong-kwun-deo</rt><rb>问</rb><rt>meng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>卖掉</rb><rt>ma-diao</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>谁</rb><rt>jü</rt></ruby>？」<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt></ruby>。<sup>21</sup> <u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>看见</rb><rt>kʽen-kyin</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就</rb><rt>ziu</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>主</rb><rt>Cü</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>人</rb><rt>nying</rt><rb>咋</rb><rt>dza</rt><rb>呢</rb><rt>ni</rt></ruby>？」<sup>22</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>于</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？<ruby><rb>尔</rb><rt>Ng</rt><rb>跟着</rb><rt>keng-djôh</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>是了</rb><rt>z-de</rt></ruby>。」<sup>23</sup> <ruby><rb>箇遭</rb><rt>Keh-tsao</rt><rb>兄弟</rb><rt>hyüong-di</rt><rb>里向</rb><rt>li-hyiang</rt><rb>有</rb><rt>yiu</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>说话</rb><rt>shih-wô</rt><rb>话开去了</rb><rt>wô-kʽæ-kyʽi-de</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>箇个</rb><rt>Keh-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>死个</rb><rt>si-go</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>但是</rb><rt>dæn-z</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>呒得</rb><rt>m̆-teh</rt><rb>等</rb><rt>teng</rt></ruby><u><ruby><rb>彼得</rb><rt>Pe-teh</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>话</rb><rt>wô</rt></ruby>，「<ruby><rb>其</rb><rt>Gyi</rt><rb>𣍐</rb><rt>ve</rt><rb>死</rb><rt>si</rt></ruby>。」<ruby><rb>是</rb><rt>*z*</rt><rb>话</rb><rt>*wô*</rt></ruby>：「<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>要</rb><rt>iao</rt><rb>其</rb><rt>gyi</rt><rb>来东</rb><rt>læ-tong</rt><rb>一直</rb><rt>ih-dzih</rt><rb>到</rb><rt>tao</rt><rb>我</rb><rt>Ngô</rt><rb>来个</rb><rt>læ-go</rt><rb>时候</rb><rt>z-ʽeo</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>于</rb><rt>yü</rt><rb>尔</rb><rt>ng</rt><rb>啥个</rb><rt>soh-go</rt><rb>相干</rb><rt>siang-ken</rt></ruby>？」

<sup>24</sup> <ruby><rb>对证</rb><rt>TE-TSING</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>写</rb><rt>sia</rt><rb>箇星</rb><rt>keh-sing</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>主顾</rb><rt>cü-kwu</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>箇个</rb><rt>keh-go</rt><rb>门徒</rb><rt>meng-du</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>阿拉</rb><rt>ah-lah</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>晓得</rb><rt>hyiao-teh</rt><rb>其个</rb><rt>gyi-go</rt><rb>对证</rb><rt>te-tsing</rt><rb>是</rb><rt>z</rt><rb>实惠个</rb><rt>jih-we-go</rt></ruby>。

<sup>25</sup> <u><ruby><rb>耶稣</rb><rt>Yiæ-su</rt></ruby></u><ruby><rb>还有</rb><rt>wa-yiu</rt><rb>许多</rb><rt>hyü-to</rt><rb>事干</rb><rt>z-ken</rt><rb>做过</rb><rt>tso-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>若是</rb><rt>ziah-z</rt><rb>一打一</rb><rt>ih-tang-ih</rt><rb>拢总</rb><rt>long-tsong</rt><rb>写落</rb><rt>sia-lôh</rt></ruby>，<ruby><rb>我</rb><rt>ngô</rt><rb>忖忖</rb><rt>tsʽeng-tsʽeng</rt><rb>写落个</rb><rt>sia-lôh-go</rt><rb>书</rb><rt>shü</rt><rb>就是</rb><rt>ziu-z</rt><rb>世间上</rb><rt>shü-kæn-zông</rt><rb>也</rb><rt>yia</rt><rb>囥弗过</rb><rt>kʽông-feh-ko</rt></ruby>。<ruby><rb>亚们</rb><rt>Üô-meng</rt></ruby>。
